{"forms":[{"form":"personal unions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The United Kingdoms of Sweden and Norway, also known variously as the United Kingdoms, Sweden and Norway or Sweden–Norway, was a personal union of the separate kingdoms of Sweden and Norway under a common monarch and common foreign policy that lasted from 1814 until its peaceful dissolution in 1905.","type":"example"},{"text":"Many personal unions were ended by differences in the monarchies' rules of dynastic succession, while disputes thereabout lead to dynastic wars.","type":"example"},{"text":"The Congo Free State was an independent kingdom in central Africa in personal union with Belgium under King Leopold.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The situation of two or more states sharing the same monarch, usually also with shared dynastic succession."],"tags":["countable","historical","uncountable"],"topics":["government","politics"]},{"glosses":["Hypostatic union; the union of Christ's humanity and divinity in one individual existence (person)."],"tags":["Christian","countable","uncountable"],"topics":["lifestyle","religion","theology"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"union of states under the same monarch","word":"composite monarchy"},{"sense":"union of states under the same monarch","word":"composite state"},{"sense":"union of Christ's human and divine natures in one person","word":"hypostatic union"}],"upstream_line":2053420,"upstream_offset":6573592072,"upstream_record_sha256":"b918cb4e9018e6767c46bf8a6315f93c5a20afac55dcfd8f365a2eb6a5481576","word":"personal union"}
